| 2026-09-22 07:20 | S11 | **Two defects that made trade-from-X impossible, found by driving it and fixed.** Neither would have been fixed by getting the X account the rows were filed as waiting on. **(1) `selectXWindow` filtered `lane === "regular"`.** No pre-IPO name is ever listed on Regular — D-103 lists them on the 24/7 token lane — so every `@… OPENAI …` mention refused `no-window` and the X rail could only trade inside a NYSE session, which is exactly the window in which nobody had driven it. The Blink path already asked the question correctly through `actionLane`; `selectXWindow` now delegates to `selectActionWindow`, so both share one rule, and the existing invariant that a *stock* never resolves onto the token lane is unchanged and still tested. **(2) `X_MONETARY_CEILING` was `(1n << 128n) - 1n`** while the deployed vault stores `max_stake_per_trade` as **u64** — IDL `CapsArgs`, `instructions/grants.rs:20`, and the state layout test pinning the field at 8 bytes. Every X grant was refused by the codec before it left the browser (`Codec [u64] expected number to be in the range [0, 18446744073709551615], got 340282366920938463463374607431768211455`), and `isBalanceOnlyXGrant` compared live grants against a value none of them could ever hold — so Authorize could not succeed and, had one existed, the relay would have answered `grant-update-required` forever. It is a u64 ceiling now, and grant 10 above reads back `18446744073709551615` on both cap fields with `isBalanceOnlyXGrant` true. **A third, operational:** an ops actor needs an **absolute** `NEXT_PUBLIC_AGARI_INDEXER_URL`; only `web/.env.local` carried it, as the browser's relative `/api/index`, so the relay answered `market-data-unavailable` to everything until its launcher set one | L-58, L-60 | (this commit) | `window.test.ts` 7/7, `x/` 28/28, typecheck + invariants green | ✅ 3 found, 3 fixed |

| 2026-09-22 21:34 | S16 | **Site-wide outage found and fixed: from the 20:00Z close every page of useagari.xyz showed the error boundary (React error 310).** Cause: `Marquee.tsx` pushed `sessionCell()`, which called the `useWhen()` hook, only once the session had loaded with the market shut, so the marquee's hook count grew on that render (introduced by `d7a17fe` this afternoon; hidden while NYSE was open). Diagnosis: headless-Chrome CDP captures with console exceptions; the crash frame was `useWhen`'s `useSyncExternalStore` in a chunk built at 19:04; all 37 chunks matched the origin, so the cache was not it. Fix `04c28b3` hoists the hook into `Marquee()`; web deployment `11eyb5hmizbgrfw2eyqsyaas` landed 21:34:30Z; captures of `/`, `/markets`, `/baskets`, `/tickers/AILABS` show 0 React errors and the marquee reading `AILABS 1,017.63 pts · DEFSPACE 1,004.25 pts · REOPENS WED 14:30` | — | 04c28b3 | CDP captures | ✅ pass |
